\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@for all $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ and $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}}$, we have $${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}}\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}})}{\enumerateSharedOp({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}})}{\enumerateSharedOp({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}})}}=\begin{cases}\unknownTerminalInOp_{b},&\text{if }{\mathchoice{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}\left(i\right)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}(i)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}(i)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}(i)}}\neq\emptyset\\
\blockedIndex,&\text{otherwise.}\end{cases}$$\end{enumerate}\end{definition}
\par In the definition above, we map all link-local indices to shared indices across all considered bold links using the functions $(\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a})_{a\in A}$.
Then, we identify each shared index $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[u\right]}{[u]}{[u]}{[u]}}$ with a terminal set ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(i\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}}$.
If the concrete terminal set is known at this point (i.e.,~it is contained in $\mathcal{X}$), we assign it to exactly this terminal set.
We ensure this with \lx@cref{creftypepluralcap~refnum}{item:local-shared-def-preserves-crossLink} and\lx@nobreakspace\lx@cref{refnum}{item:local-shared-def-number-containedInNode}.
In particular, \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{item:local-shared-def-preserves-crossLink} ensures that for all $a\in A$ and $T\in{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}}$ there is a bijection between the local and shared indices for subgraphs designated for $T$.
For terminal sets $T\in\mathcal{T}$ completely contained in $X_{s}$ (i.e.,~$T\in\mathcal{T}^{\star}_{s}$), we ensure with \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{item:local-shared-def-number-containedInNode} that the number of designated subgraphs does not exceed the number of required subgraphs.
\par However, if the terminal set is completely contained in a $\{Y_{b}\}_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}$, we do not know the exact terminal set to which this subgraph gets assigned in the solution.
This limitation stems from the fact that if we would store the concrete terminal sets, the size of the dynamic program might no longer be bounded by a function of the parameter.
Luckily, we can treat them interchangeably at this point.
So, we introduce the symbols $\{\unknownTerminalInOp_{b}\}_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}$, where for any $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ the symbol $\unknownTerminalInOp_{b}$ is used to denote an arbitrary terminal set completely contained in $Y_{b}$.
We introduce the symbol $\blockedIndex$ to symbolize that an index does not correspond to a subgraph of the solution.
This is done, in part, for $\enumerateSharedOp$ to be a total function.
Let $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$.
Using \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{item:local-shared-def-unnamed-sensible}, we ensure that each subgraph that is designated to be assigned to a terminal set contained in $Y_{b}$ crosses ${\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}}$.
The purpose of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{item:local-shared-def-unknown-is-unnamend-or-blocked} is two-fold.
First and in combination with \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{item:local-shared-def-unnamed-sensible}, we require that exactly the indices $j\in{\mathchoice{\left[u\right]}{[u]}{[u]}{[u]}}$ for which there is a $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}}$ with ${\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}}=j$ map to $\unknownTerminalInOp_{b}$.
That is ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(\unknownTerminalInOp_{b}\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\unknownTerminalInOp_{b})}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\unknownTerminalInOp_{b})}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\unknownTerminalInOp_{b})}}=\{{\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}}\mid i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}};{\mathchoice{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}\left(i\right)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}(i)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}(i)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}(i)}}\neq\emptyset\}$.
Additionally, \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{item:local-shared-def-unknown-is-unnamend-or-blocked} ensures that the remaining indices of ${\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}\left({\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}}\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}({\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}})}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}({\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}})}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}({\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}})}}$ are not used.
\par Consider the graph $J_{s}\coloneqq G^{*}_{s}\left[X_{s}\cup V({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}})\cup\{q_{s,i}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}}\right]\cup\bigcup_{c\in{\mathchoice{\childrenOp\left(s\right)}{\childrenOp(s)}{\childrenOp(s)}{\childrenOp(s)}}}{\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{c}}{\boundaryOp_{c}}{\boundaryOp_{c}}{\boundaryOp_{c}}}$, which is graph obtained from $G^{*}_{s}$ after removing all edges completely contained in a $\{Y_{b}\}_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}$ and all vertices isolated by this operation.
This graph is the part of $G_{s}$ that is most relevant for propagating the dynamic program from the bold children to their parent $s$.
For all $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ and $P\in\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\cup\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}$ we add a vertex $\tilde{p}_{b,P}$ connected to all $V(G[P])$.
The vertices $\tilde{p}_{b,P}$ can be used to simulate that the edges in $P$ are connected using a subgraph contained in $Y_{b}$.
For each $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[u\right]}{[u]}{[u]}{[u]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(\blockedIndex\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}}$ we add a vertex $\tilde{m}_{i}$ to the graph connected to all $V(J_{s})\setminus\bigcup_{t\in{\mathchoice{\thinChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\thinChildrenOp(s)}{\thinChildrenOp(s)}{\thinChildrenOp(s)}}}X_{t}$.
These vertices are used to mark the subgraphs for the particular indices.
Call the obtained graph $\widetilde{J_{s}}$.
\par First, we consider the subgraphs that are assigned a shared index $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[u\right]}{[u]}{[u]}{[u]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(\blockedIndex\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}}$.
If ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(i\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}}\in\mathcal{X}$, we set $\widetilde{C_{i}}\coloneqq{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(i\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}}\cap X_{s}$ and otherwise $\widetilde{C_{i}}\coloneqq\emptyset$, as the vertices of $X_{s}$ which need to be contained in this subgraph by the assigned terminal set.
Now, if $i\in{\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{s}\left({\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}}\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{s}({\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}})}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{s}({\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}})}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{s}({\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}})}}$, we set $\widetilde{M_{i}}\coloneqq\widetilde{C_{i}}\cup\{q_{s,{\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{i}^{-1}\left(s\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{i}^{-1}(s)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{i}^{-1}(s)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{i}^{-1}(s)}}},\tilde{m}_{i}\}$ and otherwise $\widetilde{M_{i}}\coloneqq\widetilde{C_{i}}\cup\{\tilde{m}_{i}\}$.
This ensures that all vertices of the final terminal set ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(i\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}}$ contained in $Y_{s}$ are included, that the subgraph is marked using $\tilde{m}_{i}$, and that we can simulate, if necessary, that the subgraph is connected outside of $G_{s}$.
Next, we consider the subgraphs that supply connections to the remaining graph.
These are the subgraphs crossing a bold link adjacent to $s$ that are not assigned an index.
More concretely, we consider the subgraphs witnessing that $P\in\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau}$ can be connected in $G_{s}$.
Recall, that $R_{s,P}\coloneqq V(G[P])\setminus Y_{s}$.
Finally, we consider the subgraphs that do not cross into a $G[Y_{b}]$ for any $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$.
These are the remaining subgraphs that get assigned to a $T\in\mathcal{T}^{\star}_{s}$.
For these we do not need to alter the set of terminals and set $\widetilde{\mathcal{T}^{\star}_{s}}\coloneqq\{T\in\mathcal{T}^{\star}_{s}\mid{\mathchoice{\left|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}\right|}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}}<d(T)\}$.
The terminal sets for the instance we define as $\widetilde{\mathcal{T}_{s}}\coloneqq\{\widetilde{M_{i}}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\left[u\right]}{[u]}{[u]}{[u]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(\blockedIndex\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}}}\cup\{R_{s,P}\}_{P\in\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau}}\cup\widetilde{\mathcal{T}^{\star}_{s}}$.
\par Note that the sets $\{\widetilde{M_{i}}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\left[u\right]}{[u]}{[u]}{[u]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(\blockedIndex\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}}}$, $\{R_{s,P}\}_{P\in\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau}}$, and $\widetilde{\mathcal{T}^{\star}_{s}}$ are pairwise disjoint.
For all $T\in\{\widetilde{M_{i}}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\left[u\right]}{[u]}{[u]}{[u]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(\blockedIndex\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}}}$, we set the demand to 1.
For all $T\in\{R_{s,P}\}_{P\in\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau}}$, we set the demand equal to the number of $P$ giving rise to $T$.
For all $T\in\widetilde{\mathcal{T}^{\star}_{s}}$, we set the demand to $d(T)-{\mathchoice{\left|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}\right|}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}}$.
Call the obtained instance $\mathscr{C}(s,\tau,(\tau_{b})_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}},\enumerateSharedOp,(\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a})_{a\in A})$.
\par\begin{definition}Choose for all $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ a $\tau_{b}\in D(b)$.
We say that $(\tau_{b})_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}$ witnesses $\tau\in D(s)$, if there is a local to shared mapping $(\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a})_{a\in A}$ with a shared terminal enumerator $\enumerateSharedOp$ such that there is a solution $(\widetilde{\mathcal{F}},\tilde{\pi})$ to the instance $\mathscr{C}(s,\tau,(\tau_{b})_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}},\enumerateSharedOp,(\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a})_{a\in A})$ such that
\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@we have $E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}})\cap\bigcup E(\widetilde{\mathcal{F}})\subseteq\bigcup\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau}\cup\bigcup\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau}$,
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@for all $P\in\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau}$ and $\widetilde{F}\in\tilde{\pi}^{-1}(R_{s,P})$, the set $V(\widetilde{F})$ is disjoint from $\{q_{s,i}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}}$,
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@for all $P\in\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau}$, there is a $\widetilde{F}\in\tilde{\pi}^{-1}(R_{s,P})$ with $E(\widetilde{F})\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{S}}{\boundaryOp_{S}}{\boundaryOp_{S}}{\boundaryOp_{S}}})=P$.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@for all $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}$, let $\mathcal{P}_{i}\coloneqq{\mathchoice{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau}^{-1}\left(i\right)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau}^{-1}(i)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau}^{-1}(i)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau}^{-1}(i)}}$, then
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@if $\mathcal{P}_{i}=\emptyset$, we have $q_{s,i}\notin\bigcup V(\mathcal{\widetilde{F}})$,
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@otherwise, there is exactly one $\widetilde{F}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{F}}$ with $q_{s,i}\in V(\widetilde{F})$ and this $\widetilde{F}$ additionally satisfies that $E(\widetilde{F}-q_{s,i})$ can be partitioned into $\{\widetilde{E}_{P}\}_{P\in\mathcal{P}_{i}}$ such that for all $P\in\mathcal{P}_{i}$, the graph $\widetilde{F}[\widetilde{E}_{P}]$ is connected and $\widetilde{E}_{P}\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}})=P$, and for all $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ and $P^{\prime}\in\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\cup\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}$, there is at most one $P\in\mathcal{P}$ with $\tilde{p}_{b,P^{\prime}}\in V(\widetilde{F}[\widetilde{E}_{P}])$, additionally this $P$ satisfies $P^{\prime}\subseteq E_{P}$.
\end{itemize}\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@for all $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[u\right]}{[u]}{[u]}{[u]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(\blockedIndex\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}}$, let $\{\widetilde{F}\}\coloneqq\tilde{\pi}^{-1}(\widetilde{M_{i}})$, then
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@there is at most one edge adjacent to $m_{i}$ in $\bigcup E(\mathcal{\widetilde{F}})$,
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@for all $a\in A$ with ${\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}^{-1}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(i)}}\neq\emptyset$ and $P\in{\mathchoice{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}\left({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(i)}}\right)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(i)}})}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(i)}})}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(i)}})}}$, we have $P\subseteq E(\widetilde{F})$ and if $a\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ even $\tilde{p}_{a,P}\in V(\widetilde{F})$.
\end{itemize}\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@for all $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ and $P\in\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\cup\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}$, there is at most one $\widetilde{F}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{F}}$ with $\tilde{p}_{b,P}\in V(\widetilde{F})$ and this particular $\widetilde{F}$ satisfies $P\subseteq E(\widetilde{F})$.
\end{enumerate}\end{definition}
\par To understand the definition above, consider a solution $(\mathcal{F},\pi)$ to the instance $\mathscr{D}_{s,\tau}$.
This solution induces for all $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ a solution $(\mathcal{F}_{b},\pi_{b})$ to sub-instances $\mathscr{D}_{b,\tau_{b}}$, where $\tau_{b}$ is an element of $D(b)$.
Additionally, $(\mathcal{F},\pi)$ induces a solution $(\widetilde{F},\tilde{\pi})$ to $\mathscr{C}(s,\tau,(\tau_{b})_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}},\enumerateSharedOp,(\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a})_{a\in A})$.
We see that \lx@cref{creftypepluralcap~refnum}{item:simple-tcd-witness-edge-partitions}, \lx@cref{refnum}{item:simple-tcd-witness-future-sensible} and\lx@nobreakspace\lx@cref{refnum}{item:simple-tcd-witness-future-exact} of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcd-witness} are direct adaptations of \lx@cref{creftypepluralcap~refnum}{item:simple-tcd-edge-partitions}, \lx@cref{refnum}{item:simple-tcd-future-sensible} and\lx@nobreakspace\lx@cref{refnum}{item:simple-tcd-future-exact} of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcw-dp} transferred from $(\mathcal{F},\pi)$ to $(\widetilde{F},\tilde{\pi})$.
\lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{item:simple-tcd-witness-sub-connected} of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcd-witness} ensures that the connections simulated by the vertices $\tilde{p}_{b,P}$ are only used once.
This is also reflected in the change of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{item:simple-tcd-witness-past} in \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcd-witness}.
We adapt it to make sure that the connections simulated by the vertices $\tilde{p}_{b,P}$ are only used for one subgraph giving the connections for a single $P^{\prime}\in\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau}$.
With \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{item:simple-tcd-witness-marked} of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcd-witness}, we ensure that the vertices marking the subgraphs are not used to introduce additional connections, and that the edges and connections inside a $\{G[Y_{b}]\}_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}$ which are assigned to this index are all used in the marked subgraph.
\par We now show in \lx@cref{creftypepluralcap~refnum}{stmt:simple-tcd-dp-implies-witness} and\lx@nobreakspace\lx@cref{refnum}{stmt:simple-tcd-dp-implies-witness}, that \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcd-witness} fully captures whether $\tau\in D(s)$.
\par\begin{lemma}Let $\tau\in D(s)$.
Then, for all $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ there is a $\tau_{b}\in D(b)$ such that $(\tau_{b})_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}$ witnesses $\tau\in D(s)$.
\end{lemma}
\@proof As $\tau\in D(s)$, there is a solution $(\mathcal{F},\pi)$ to $\mathscr{D}_{s,\tau}$ satisfying the additional requirements of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcw-dp}.
First, we define the shared terminal enumerator $\enumerateSharedOp$ and a shared labelling of the solution subgraphs containing an edge of $\bigcup_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})$.
Then, we define the $(\tau_{b})_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}$ using local labellings of the solution subgraphs using an edge of $E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})$.
Based on the last two results, we then define the local to shared mappings $(\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a})_{a\in A}$ and prove that the shared terminal enumerator defined previously actually fulfills the requirements of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:local-shared-map}.
Finally, we show that the $(\tau_{b})_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}$ satisfy the requirements of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcd-witness}.
\par Let $\mathcal{F}^{\prime}\coloneqq\{F\in\mathcal{F}\mid E(F)\cap E(J_{s})\neq\emptyset\}$ be the solution subgraphs containing an edge of $J_{s}$.
These are the subgraphs relevant for propagating the dynamic program.
Denote with $\mathcal{F}^{\star}\coloneqq\{F\in\mathcal{F}^{\prime}\mid V(F)\subseteq X_{s}\cup\bigcup_{t\in{\mathchoice{\thinChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\thinChildrenOp(s)}{\thinChildrenOp(s)}{\thinChildrenOp(s)}}}X_{t}\}$ the solution subgraphs that are completely contained in $X_{s}$ and the vertices of the thin children of $s$.
These subgraphs are very local and not of high importance for the propagation of the dynamic program.
We can disregard them for most of the following arguments.
Let $\mathcal{F}^{\boundaryOp}\coloneqq\mathcal{F}^{\prime}\setminus\mathcal{F}^{\star}$ be the remaining subgraphs, which cross the boundary of $s$ or a bold child.
Partition $\mathcal{F}^{\boundaryOp}$ by whether or not the subgraph is designated for a terminal set starting above or below $s$ in the final solution, that is $F^{\uparrow}\coloneqq\bigcup_{P\in\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau}}\pi^{-1}(R_{s,P})\subseteq\mathcal{F}^{\boundaryOp}$ and $\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}\coloneqq\mathcal{F}^{\boundaryOp}\setminus\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}$.
The tree-cut decomposition is simple and therefore friendly; so, $s$ has at most $w+2$ bold children and $\ell\coloneqq{\mathchoice{\left|\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}\right|}{{|\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}|}}{{|\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}|}}{{|\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}|}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\left|\mathcal{F}^{\boundaryOp}\right|}{{|\mathcal{F}^{\boundaryOp}|}}{{|\mathcal{F}^{\boundaryOp}|}}{{|\mathcal{F}^{\boundaryOp}|}}}\leq w(w+3)=u-w$.
So, let $\sigma\colon\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}\to{\mathchoice{\left[\ell\right]}{[\ell]}{[\ell]}{[\ell]}}$ be a bijection, which gives a shared numbering on the subgraphs crossing bold links.
\par Now, we define the shared mapping enumerator.
For all $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[u\right]}{[u]}{[u]}{[u]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\left[\ell\right]}{[\ell]}{[\ell]}{[\ell]}}$, we set ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(i\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}}\coloneqq\blockedIndex$.
Let $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[\ell\right]}{[\ell]}{[\ell]}{[\ell]}}$ and $F\coloneqq\sigma^{-1}(i)$.
If there is a $j\in{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}}$ with $\pi(F)=Q_{s,j}$, then set ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(i\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}}\coloneqq{\mathchoice{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}\left(j\right)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}(j)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}(j)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}(j)}}$.
If there is a $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ with $\pi(F)\subseteq Y_{b}$, then set ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(i\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}}\coloneqq\unknownTerminalInOp_{b}$.
Otherwise, set ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(i\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}}\coloneqq\pi(F)$.
At this point it suffices to show that
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@$\enumerateSharedOp$ is actually well defined, that is that all values are contained in ${\mathchoice{\codomainOp\left(\enumerateSharedOp\right)}{\codomainOp(\enumerateSharedOp)}{\codomainOp(\enumerateSharedOp)}{\codomainOp(\enumerateSharedOp)}}=\mathcal{X}\cup\{\unknownTerminalInOp_{b}\}_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}$,
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@for all $T\in\mathcal{X}\setminus\mathcal{T}^{\star}_{s}$, we have ${\mathchoice{\left|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}\right|}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}}=d(T)$,
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@for all $F\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}$, with ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(\sigma(F)\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}}\in\mathcal{X}$, we have $\pi(F)\cap Y_{s}={\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(\sigma(F)\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}}\cap Y_{s}$.
\end{itemize}
\par For all $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}}$, we have ${\mathchoice{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}\left(i\right)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}(i)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}(i)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}(i)}}\in{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}}\subseteq\mathcal{X}$.
Now, consider an $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[\ell\right]}{[\ell]}{[\ell]}{[\ell]}}$ with $F\coloneqq\sigma^{-1}(i)$ such that there is no $j\in{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}}$ with $Q_{s,j}=\pi(F)$ and no $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ with $\pi(F)\subseteq Y_{b}$, that is the last case of the definition of $\enumerateSharedOp$ applies.
As $F\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}$, there is no $P\in\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau}$ with $F\in\pi^{-1}(R_{s,P})$.
So, $\pi(F)\in\mathcal{U}_{s}$.
If $\pi(F)\subseteq X_{s}$, then $\pi(F)\in\mathcal{T}^{\star}_{s}\subseteq\mathcal{X}$.
Otherwise, there is a $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ with $\pi(F)\cap Y_{b}\neq\emptyset$.
As $\pi(F)\not\subseteq Y_{b}$, we have $\pi(F)\in{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}}\subseteq\mathcal{X}$.
Therefore, $\enumerateSharedOp$ is well defined.
\par Now, let $T\in{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}}$.
For all $i\in{\mathchoice{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}^{-1}(T)}}$, there is a subgraph $F_{s,i}\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}$ with $q_{s,i}\in V(F_{s,i})$.
So, ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(\sigma^{-1}(F_{s,i})\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma^{-1}(F_{s,i}))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma^{-1}(F_{s,i}))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma^{-1}(F_{s,i}))}}=T$, showing ${\mathchoice{\left|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}\right|}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}}\geq d(T)$.
By construction, no other solution subgraphs $F^{\prime}$ exhibit ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(\sigma^{-1}(F^{\prime})\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma^{-1}(F^{\prime}))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma^{-1}(F^{\prime}))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma^{-1}(F^{\prime}))}}=T$.
So, ${\mathchoice{\left|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}\right|}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}}=d(T)$.
Now, let $T\in\mathcal{X}\setminus({\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}}\cup\mathcal{T}^{\star}_{s})$.
Then, $T\in\mathcal{U}_{s}$ and we see that $\pi^{-1}(T)\subseteq\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}$ and so ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}=\pi^{-1}(T)$.
Thus, ${\mathchoice{\left|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}\right|}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}}=d(T)$.
\par Consider $F\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}$ with ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(\sigma(F)\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}}\in\mathcal{X}$.
If there is a $j\in{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(s)}}]}}$ with $Q_{s,j}=\pi(F)$, then $\pi(F)\cap Y_{s}={\mathchoice{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}\left(j\right)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}(j)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}(j)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}(j)}}\cap Y_{s}={\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(\sigma(F)\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}}\cap Y_{s}$.
Otherwise, we have $\pi(F)\in\mathcal{U}_{s}$; so, $\pi(F)={\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(\sigma(F)\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}}$, which is sufficient to prove the claim.
\par Let $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$, we now define $\tau_{b}$.
Let $\mathcal{F}_{b}\coloneq\{F\in\mathcal{F}\mid E(F)\cap E(G_{b})\neq\emptyset\}$ be the trees that cross into $G_{b}=G[Y_{b}]\cup{\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}}$ and let $\mathcal{F}_{b}^{\boundaryOp}\coloneqq\{F\in\mathcal{F}_{b}\mid E(F)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})\neq\emptyset\}$ be the trees of $\mathcal{F}_{b}$ that are not completely contained in $G[Y_{b}]$.
Let $\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{b}\coloneqq\{{F\in\mathcal{F}^{\boundaryOp}_{b}}\mid\pi(F)\cap Y_{b}\neq\emptyset\}$ be the trees of $\mathcal{F}^{\boundaryOp}_{b}$ that are assigned to terminal sets starting below the link between $b$ and its parent and let $\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{b}\coloneqq\mathcal{F}^{\boundaryOp}_{b}\setminus\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{b}$ be the remaining trees crossing ${\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}}$.
Recall that the terminal sets of $\mathscr{D}_{s,\tau}$ can be partitioned into $\mathcal{U}_{s}$, $\{R_{s,P}\}_{P\in\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau}}$, and $\{Q_{s,i}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}}}$.
We note that for all $P\in\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau}$, we have by definition that $R_{s,P}$ is disjoint from $Y_{s}\supseteq Y_{b}$ and so $\pi^{-1}(R_{s,P})$ is disjoint from $F^{\downarrow}_{b}$.
Thus, $F^{\downarrow}_{b}$ are the subgraphs that, by our interpretation of the dynamic program, get assigned to terminal sets starting at or below $b$ in our final solution.
\par We set $\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\coloneqq\bigcup_{F\in\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{b}}\{E(K)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})\mid K\in{\mathchoice{\compOp\left(F[E(G_{b})]\right)}{\compOp(F[E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[E(G_{b})])}}\}$ to be the edge sets of the components of the solution subgraphs of $\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{b}$ after removing all edges outside $G_{s}$ and all vertices isolated by this operation.
\par To define $\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}$ and $\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}$, we define a local numbering $\lambda_{b}\colon\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{b}\to{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}$ where $\lambda_{b}$ is an injective function.
Let $T\in{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}}$, and set $\mathcal{F}_{T}\coloneqq\sigma^{-1}({\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}})$.
Notice that $\mathcal{F}_{T}\subseteq\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{b}$ and that ${\mathchoice{\left|\mathcal{F}_{T}\right|}{{|\mathcal{F}_{T}|}}{{|\mathcal{F}_{T}|}}{{|\mathcal{F}_{T}|}}}={\mathchoice{\left|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}\right|}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}}=d(T)$.
This enables us to choose for each $F\in\mathcal{F}_{T}$ a unique $i\in{\mathchoice{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(T)}}$ and set $\lambda_{b}(F)\coloneqq i$.
Note that $\sigma^{-1}({\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}})=\lambda^{-1}_{b}({\mathchoice{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(T)}})$, that is we designate exactly the same solution subgraphs to $T$ in the solution $\mathscr{D}_{b,\tau_{b}}$ as in the solution to $\mathscr{D}_{s,\tau}$.
Then, we choose for each $F\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{b}\setminus\bigcup_{T\in{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}}}\mathcal{F}_{T}$ a unique $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}}$ and set $\lambda_{b}(F)\coloneqq i$, concluding the description of $\lambda_{b}$.
\par Now, consider $F\in\mathcal{F}$.
If there is a $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}$ such that $q_{s,i}\in V(F)$, let $\mathcal{P}\coloneqq{\mathchoice{\pastAssignBaseOp_{s}^{-1}\left(i\right)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{s}^{-1}(i)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{s}^{-1}(i)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{s}^{-1}(i)}}$.
By \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{item:simple-tcd-past} of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcw-dp}, we can partition $E(F-q_{s,i})$ into $\mathcal{E}_{F}\coloneqq\{E_{P}\}_{P\in\mathcal{P}}$ such that for all $P\in\mathcal{P}$, the graph $F[E_{P}]$ is connected and $E_{P}\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}})=P$.
Otherwise, set $\mathcal{E}_{F}\coloneqq\{E(F)\}$.
Note that for all $F\in\mathcal{F}$, we have $\bigcup\mathcal{E}_{F}=E(F-\{q_{s,i}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}})$.
We now set $\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\coloneqq\bigcup_{F\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{b},D\in\mathcal{E}_{F}}\{E(K)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})\mid K\in{\mathchoice{\compOp\left(F[D\cap E(G_{b})]\right)}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}}\}$, that is we consider for each $F\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{b}$ the partitioning $\mathcal{E}_{F}$ of the edge set of the graph $F-\{q_{s,i}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}}$ given by \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{item:simple-tcd-past} and for each $D\in\mathcal{E}_{F}$ and each connected component $K$ of $F[D]$ with all edges outside $G_{b}$ removed, we create a partition in $\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}$ containing the edges of $K$ in ${\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}}$.
Finally, for all $F\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{b},D\in\mathcal{E}_{F}$ and $K\in{\mathchoice{\compOp\left(F[D\cap E(G_{b})]\right)}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}}$ we set ${\mathchoice{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\left(E(K)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})\right)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}(E(K)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}}))}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}(E(K)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}}))}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}(E(K)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}}))}}\coloneqq\lambda_{b}(F)$.
Note that we have $\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}=\bigcup_{F\in\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow},D\in\mathcal{E}_{F}}\{E(K)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})\mid K\in{\mathchoice{\compOp\left(F[D\cap E(G_{b})]\right)}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}}\}$.
\par We now show that $\tau_{b}\in D(b)$.
For this, we need to provide a solution to $\mathscr{D}_{b,\tau_{b}}$ satisfying the additional requirements of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcw-dp}.
First, consider a $F\in\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{b}$ and let $K\in{\mathchoice{\compOp\left(F[E(G_{b})]\right)}{\compOp(F[E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[E(G_{b})])}}$.
In our solution, we assign $K$ to the terminal set $V(K)\setminus Y_{b}=R_{b,E(K)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})}$.
Note that this already satisfies \lx@cref{creftypepluralcap~refnum}{item:simple-tcd-future-exact} and\lx@nobreakspace\lx@cref{refnum}{item:simple-tcd-future-sensible} of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcw-dp}.
Additionally, it is easy to see that the demand of all $\{R_{b,P}\}_{P\in\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}}$ is satisfied by this assignment.
\par Now, consider $F\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{b}\subseteq\mathcal{F}^{\boundaryOp}_{b}$.
Let $F^{\prime}\coloneqq F[E(G_{s})]$ which might be disconnected.
Note that any $K\in{\mathchoice{\compOp\left(F^{\prime}\right)}{\compOp(F^{\prime})}{\compOp(F^{\prime})}{\compOp(F^{\prime})}}$ contains a $v_{K}\in V({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})\setminus Y_{b}$.
Let $F^{*}_{\lambda_{b}(F)}$ be $F^{\prime}$ combined with $q_{b,\lambda_{b}(F)}$ and all incident edges.
As $q_{b,\lambda_{b}(F)}$ is connected to a vertex in each component of $F^{\prime}$, the graph $F^{*}_{\lambda_{b}(F)}$ is connected and as the underlying $F\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{b}$ are edge-disjoint and as $\lambda_{b}$ is injective, all such $F^{*}_{\lambda_{b}(F)}$ are edge-disjoint.
Notice that $F^{*}_{\lambda_{b}(F)}$ is contained in $G^{*}_{b}$.
\par Let $T\coloneqq{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(\sigma(F)\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}}$.
As $F\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{b}$, we have $\pi(F)\cap Y_{b}\neq\emptyset$.
If $T\in{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}}$, we have $\lambda_{b}(F)\in{\mathchoice{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}^{-1}(T)}}$; so, $V(F^{*}_{\lambda_{b}(F)})\supseteq({\mathchoice{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}\left(\lambda_{b}(F)\right)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}}\cap Y_{b})\cup\{q_{b,\lambda_{b}(F)}\}=Q_{b,\lambda_{b}(F)}$.
Consequently, we assign $F^{*}_{\lambda_{b}(F)}$ to $Q_{b,\lambda_{b}(F)}$.
If $T\in\mathcal{X}\setminus{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}}$, we have $T\cap Y_{b}=\pi(F)\cap Y_{b}\neq\emptyset$, but since $T\notin{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}}$, we have $T\setminus Y_{b}=\emptyset$.
Thus, $T\notin\mathcal{U}_{b}$, violating $T\in\mathcal{X}$.
Finally, assume there is a $b^{\prime}\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ with $T=\unknownTerminalInOp_{b^{\prime}}$.
As $\pi(F)\cap Y_{b}\neq\emptyset$, we have $b^{\prime}=b$ and $\pi(F)\subseteq Y_{b}$.
So, we assign $F^{*}_{\lambda_{b}(F)}$ to $\pi(F)\in\mathcal{U}_{b}$ in our solution to $\mathscr{D}_{b,\tau_{b}}$.
\par Assigning the $\{F^{*}_{i}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\imageOp\left(\lambda_{b}\right)}{\imageOp(\lambda_{b})}{\imageOp(\lambda_{b})}{\imageOp(\lambda_{b})}}}$ as described satisfies the demand of all $\{Q_{b,i}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}}}$ and for all $U\in\mathcal{U}_{b}$, this assigns ${\mathchoice{\left|\{F\in\pi^{-1}(U)\mid E(F)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})\neq\emptyset\}\right|}{{|\{F\in\pi^{-1}(U)\mid E(F)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})\neq\emptyset\}|}}{{|\{F\in\pi^{-1}(U)\mid E(F)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})\neq\emptyset\}|}}{{|\{F\in\pi^{-1}(U)\mid E(F)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})\neq\emptyset\}|}}}$ subgraphs to $U$.
Let $U\in\mathcal{U}_{b}$, and consider a $F\in\pi^{-1}(U)$ with $E(F)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}})=\emptyset$.
As $U\subseteq Y_{b}$ and $F\notin\mathcal{F}^{\boundaryOp}_{b}$, the graph $F$ is completely contained in $G[Y_{b}]$; so, we assign $F$ to $U$ is our solution.
This satisfies the requirements of all $\mathcal{U}$.
Which completes the description of our solution to $\mathscr{D}_{b,\tau_{b}}$.
\par To complete the prove that $\tau_{b}\in D(b)$, we need to show that our solution satisfies the additional requirements posed in \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcw-dp}.
Note that by construction \lx@cref{creftypepluralcap~refnum}{item:simple-tcd-future-sensible} and\lx@nobreakspace\lx@cref{refnum}{item:simple-tcd-future-exact} are already satisfied.
We see that the edges of ${\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}}$ that are used in our solution are exactly $E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})\cap\bigcup E(\mathcal{F}^{\boundaryOp}_{b})$.
By definition of $\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}$ and $\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}$, we have $\bigcup\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}=E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})\cap\bigcup E(\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{b})$ and $\bigcup\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}=E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})\cap\bigcup E(\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{b})$.
So, $\bigcup\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\cup\bigcup\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}=E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})\cap\bigcup E(\mathcal{F}^{\boundaryOp}_{b})$ showing that \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{item:simple-tcd-edge-partitions} of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcw-dp} is satisfied.
\par Now, consider $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}$ and let $\mathcal{P}_{i}\coloneqq{\mathchoice{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}\left(i\right)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}(i)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}(i)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}^{-1}(i)}}$.
If $P_{i}=\emptyset$, there is no $F\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{b}$ with $\lambda_{b}(F)=i$ and by construction there is no solution subgraph $F$ in our solution with $q_{b,i}\in V(F)$.
Otherwise, we have that $q_{b,i}\in V(F^{*}_{i})$.
The subgraph $F^{*}_{i}$ is part of our solution and the unique subgraph of our solution containing $q_{i}$.
Additionally, $F^{*}_{i}-q_{b,i}=F[E(G_{s})]$ and $\mathcal{P}_{i}=\bigcup_{D\in\mathcal{E}_{F}}\{E(K)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})\mid K\in{\mathchoice{\compOp\left(F[D\cap E(G_{b})]\right)}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}}\}$.
Let $P\in\mathcal{P}_{i}$.
Then, there is a $D\in\mathcal{E}_{F}$ and $K\in{\mathchoice{\compOp\left(F[D\cap E(G_{b})]\right)}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}}$ with $P=E(K)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})$.
Set $E_{P}^{F}\coloneqq E(K)$.
Notice that for all $P^{\prime}\in\mathcal{P}_{i}$, the set $E^{F}_{P}$ is disjoint from $E^{F}_{P^{\prime}}$.
Additionally, $\bigcup_{P\in\mathcal{P}_{i}}E^{F}_{P}=\bigcup_{D\in\mathcal{E}_{F}}D\cap E(G_{b})=E(G_{b})\cap\bigcup_{D\in\mathcal{E}_{F}}D=E(G_{b})\cap E(F-\{q_{s,i}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}})=E(F^{*}_{i}-q_{b,i})$.
So, $\{E^{F}_{P}\}_{P\in\mathcal{P}_{i}}$ satisfies the requirements of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{item:simple-tcd-past} in \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcw-dp} and $\tau_{b}\in D(b)$.
\par We now show, that $(\tau_{b})_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}$ witness $\tau\in D(s)$.
For this, we first define a local to shared mapping $(\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a})_{a\in A}$.
We aim to use the local mappings $(\lambda_{b})_{b\in B}$ and the shared numbering $\sigma$ of the trees in $F^{\downarrow}$.
To use the same approach for $s$ as for the bold children, we now define a local numbering for $s$.
Let $\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{s}\coloneqq\{F\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}\mid E(F)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}})\neq\emptyset\}$ be the trees assigned to a terminal set starting at or below $s$ crossing ${\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}}$, analogously to $\{\mathcal{F}\}_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}$.
We now choose an injection $\lambda_{s}\colon\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{s}\to{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}$.
Let $F\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{s}$.
By \lx@cref{creftypepluralcap~refnum}{item:simple-tcd-edge-partitions}, \lx@cref{refnum}{item:simple-tcd-future-exact} and\lx@nobreakspace\lx@cref{refnum}{item:simple-tcd-past} of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcw-dp}, there is a unique $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}$ such that $q_{s,i}\in V(F)$.
Set $\lambda_{s}(F)\coloneqq i$.
Note that analogously to $\{\lambda_{b}\}_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}$, we have for all $T\in{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}}$ that $\sigma^{-1}({\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}})=\lambda^{-1}_{s}({\mathchoice{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{s}^{-1}(T)}})$.
To define the local to shared mappings, let $a\in A$.
For all $i\in{\mathchoice{\imageOp\left(\lambda_{a}\right)}{\imageOp(\lambda_{a})}{\imageOp(\lambda_{a})}{\imageOp(\lambda_{a})}}$, we set ${\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}(i)}}\coloneqq\sigma(\lambda^{-1}_{a}(i))$.
For each $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\imageOp\left(\lambda_{a}\right)}{\imageOp(\lambda_{a})}{\imageOp(\lambda_{a})}{\imageOp(\lambda_{a})}}$, we choose a unique $j\in{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(\blockedIndex\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\blockedIndex)}}={\mathchoice{\left[u\right]}{[u]}{[u]}{[u]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\left[\ell\right]}{[\ell]}{[\ell]}{[\ell]}}$, which works since $u-l\geq w$, and set ${\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}(i)}}\coloneqq j$.
\par We now show, that $(\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a})_{a\in A}$ combined with $\enumerateSharedOp$ are actually a local to shared mapping and a shared mapping enumerator according to \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:local-shared-map}.
Let $a\in A$ and $T\in{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(a\right)}{\crossLinkOp(a)}{\crossLinkOp(a)}{\crossLinkOp(a)}}$.
We know $\sigma^{-1}({\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}})=\lambda^{-1}({\mathchoice{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}})$.
Thus, ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}=\sigma(\lambda^{-1}({\mathchoice{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}}))={\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}\left({\mathchoice{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}}\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}({\mathchoice{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}})}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}({\mathchoice{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}})}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a}({\mathchoice{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateCrossLinkBaseOp_{a}^{-1}(T)}})}}$.
Consider $T\in\mathcal{T}^{\star}_{s}\subseteq\mathcal{X}$.
We have $\sigma^{-1}({\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}})\subseteq\pi^{-1}(T)$.
Thus, ${\mathchoice{\left|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}\right|}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}{{|{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(T\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(T)}}|}}}\leq d(T)$.
Let $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$.
Consider $i\in{\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}\left(\unknownTerminalInOp_{b}\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\unknownTerminalInOp_{b})}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\unknownTerminalInOp_{b})}{\enumerateSharedOp^{-1}(\unknownTerminalInOp_{b})}}$ and let $F\coloneqq\sigma^{-1}(i)$.
For all $j\in{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(s\right)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}{\crossLinkOp(s)}}]}}$, we have that $\pi(F)\neq Q_{s,j}$.
Thus, $\pi(F)\in\mathcal{U}_{s}$ and, in particular, $\pi(F)\in\mathcal{U}_{b}$, implying $F\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{b}$.
Now, assume $\lambda_{b}(F)\in{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}}$.
Then, ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(i\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}{\enumerateSharedOp(i)}}\in{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}}$, violating $\pi(F)\in\mathcal{U}_{b}$.
Thus, $\lambda_{b}(F)\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}}$ and $i=\sigma(F)=\sigma(\lambda^{-1}_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))))={\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(\lambda_{b}(F)\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}}\in{\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left({\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}}\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}({\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}})}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}({\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLink Finally, let $i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}\setminus{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\demandCrossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}{\demandCrossLinkOp(b)}}]}}$.
If $i\in{\mathchoice{\imageOp\left(\lambda_{b}\right)}{\imageOp(\lambda_{b})}{\imageOp(\lambda_{b})}{\imageOp(\lambda_{b})}}$, let $F\coloneqq\lambda^{-1}_{b}(i)\in\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{b}$, $D\in\mathcal{E}_{F}$ and $K\in{\mathchoice{\compOp\left(F[D\cap E(G_{b})]\right)}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}}$ be a component of the solution subgraph $F$ after removing all edges outside $D\cap E(G_{b})$ and all vertices that got isolated that way.
By definition ${\mathchoice{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\left(E(K)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}})\right)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}(E(K)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}}))}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}(E(K)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}}))}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}(E(K)\cap E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}}))}}=i$.
Additionally, ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(\sigma(F)\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}}\notin{\mathchoice{\crossLinkOp\left(b\right)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}{\crossLinkOp(b)}}$ and as $\pi(F)\cap Y_{b}\neq\emptyset$, we have ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(\sigma(F)\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(F))}}=\unknownTerminalInOp_{b}$ and, in particular, ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left(\sigma(\lambda^{-1}_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F)))\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(\lambda^{-1}_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(\lambda^{-1}_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))))}{\enumerateSharedOp(\sigma(\lambda^{-1}_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))))}}={\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(\lambda_{b}(F)\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}}\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(\lambda_{b}(F)\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}})}{\enumerateSharedOp({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(\lambda_{b}(F)\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}})}{\enumerateSharedOp({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(\lambda_{b}(F)\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(\lambda_{b}(F))}})}}={\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}}\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}})}{\enumerateSharedOp({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}})}{\enumerateSharedOp({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}})}}=\unknownTerminalInOp_{b}$.
If $i\notin{\mathchoice{\imageOp\left(\lambda_{b}\right)}{\imageOp(\lambda_{b})}{\imageOp(\lambda_{b})}{\imageOp(\lambda_{b})}}$, then, by construction, there is no $P\in\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}$ with ${\mathchoice{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\left(P\right)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}(P)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}(P)}{\pastAssignBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}(P)}}=i$ and ${\mathchoice{\enumerateSharedOp\left({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}}\right)}{\enumerateSharedOp({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}})}{\enumerateSharedOp({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}})}{\enumerateSharedOp({\mathchoice{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}\left(i\right)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}{\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b}(i)}})}}=\blockedIndex$,completing the proof that $(\localSharedMapBaseOp_{a})_{a\in A}$ is a local to shared mapping and $\enumerateSharedOp$ a shared mapping enumerator.
\par To prove that $(\tau_{b})_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}$ witness $\tau\in D(s)$, we provide a solution to the instance $\mathscr{C}(s,\tau,(\tau_{b})_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}},\enumerateSharedOp,(\localSharedMapBaseOp_{b})_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}})$.
For this, consider any $F\in\mathcal{F}^{\prime}=\{F\in\mathcal{F}\mid E(F)\cap E(J_{s})\neq\emptyset\}$.
We now show how to transform $F$ into a connected subgraph $\widetilde{F}$ of $\widetilde{J_{s}}$ such that the edges and vertices in $J_{s}$ do not change that much.
Let $F^{\prime}\coloneqq F[E(J_{s})]$ be $F$ with all edges outside $J_{s}$ removed and all vertices which get isolated by this operation removed.
This graph might be disconnected.
For all $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ and $P\in\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\cup\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}$ with $E(F^{\prime})\cap P\neq\emptyset$, add $\tilde{p}_{b,P}$ and all incident edges to $F^{\prime}$.
If there is a $q_{s,i}\in F^{\prime}$, add the edges $\{vq_{s,i}\mid v\in N(q_{s,i})\}$.
Call the obtained graph $\widetilde{F}$.
Notice that $V(\widetilde{F})\cap(X_{s}\cup V({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}})\cup\{q_{s,i}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}})=V(F)\cap(X_{s}\cup V({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}{\boundaryOp_{s}}})\cup\{q_{s,i}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}})$ as well as $E(\widetilde{F}-\{q_{s,i}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}})\cap E(J_{s})=E(F-\{q_{s,i}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}})\cap E(J_{s})$.
Additionally, note that $P\subseteq E(F^{\prime})\subseteq E(\widetilde{F})$, which shows that \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{item:simple-tcd-witness-sub-connected} of \lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{def:simple-tcd-witness} is fulfilled if we do not use any $\{\tilde{p}_{b,P}\}_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}},P\in\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\cup\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}}$ in another solution subgraph.
By \lx@cref{creftypepluralcap~refnum}{item:simple-tcd-future-exact} and\lx@nobreakspace\lx@cref{refnum}{item:simple-tcd-witness-past}, we also know that all such $\widetilde{F}$ are edge-disjoint.
\par To show that $\widetilde{F}$ is connected, we first show that each connected component of $\widetilde{F}$ contains an edge of $E(J_{s})$.
Then, we show—a slightly stronger statement as—that for all $D\in\mathcal{E}_{F}$, all vertices of $\widetilde{F}[D]$ are contained in one connected component of $\widetilde{F}$.
Finally, we show that there is a single vertex contained in every connected component, which shows that there is at most one connected component.
\par First, assume there is a component of $K\in{\mathchoice{\compOp\left(\widetilde{F}\right)}{\compOp(\widetilde{F})}{\compOp(\widetilde{F})}{\compOp(\widetilde{F})}}$ with $E(K)\cap E(J_{s})=\emptyset$.
If there is a $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ and $P\in\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\cup\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}$ with $\tilde{p}_{b,P}\in V(K)$, then $N[\tilde{p}_{b,P}]\subseteq V(K)$.
As $P\subseteq E(\widetilde{F}[N[p_{b,P}]])$, we have $\emptyset\neq P\subseteq E(K)$ and since $P\subseteq E(J_{s})$, this is not possible.
As $V(\widetilde{F})\subseteq V(J_{s})\cup\{\tilde{p}_{b,P}\}_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}},P\in\bigcup\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\cup\bigcup\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}}$, the graph $K$ is equal to an isolated node of $J_{s}$.
As $F$ has no isolated vertices and since $F^{\prime}$ is an edge-induced graph on $F$, the graph $F^{\prime}$ and hence $\widetilde{F}$ does not contain isolated vertices, making this impossible as well.
Thus, $E(K)$ is not disjoint from $J_{s}$.
\par Let $D\in\mathcal{E}_{F}$.
Let $\widetilde{Z}\coloneqq\{\tilde{p}_{b,P}\mid b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}},P\in\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\cup\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\}$ to be all vertices that simulate connectivity of partitions of the sub-solutions $\{\tau_{b}\}_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}$.
Let $\widetilde{Z}_{D}\coloneqq\{\tilde{p}_{b,P}\in Z\mid P\cap D\neq\emptyset\}$ be the vertices simulating connectivity inside a component for a partition induced by $D$.
Set $\widetilde{Z}^{E}_{D}\coloneqq\{uz\mid z\in Z_{D},u\in N(z)\}$ to be the edges adjacent to the vertices of $Z_{D}$.
Note that $\widetilde{Z}^{E}_{D}\subseteq E(\widetilde{F})$.
We now show, that $\widetilde{F}[D\cup\widetilde{Z}^{E}_{D}]$ is connected.
First, we note that any connected component of $\widetilde{F}[D\cup\widetilde{Z}^{E}_{D}]$ contains a vertex of $\widetilde{F}[D]$.
Consider as $u,v\in V(\widetilde{F}[D])$ vertices in different connected components of $\widetilde{F}[D\cup\widetilde{Z}^{E}_{D}]$ such that their distance in $F[D]$ is minimized.
Let $P$ be a shortest path connecting $u$ and $v$ in $F[D]$.
By minimality and since $V(F[D])\cap\{q_{s,i}\}_{i\in{\mathchoice{\left[w\right]}{[w]}{[w]}{[w]}}}=\emptyset$, we have $V(P)\cap V(J_{s})=\{u,v\}$.
Note that the sets $\{Y_{b}\setminus V(J_{s})\}_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}$ are disconnected from each other in $G^{*}_{s}$ and $V(G^{*}_{s})\setminus V(J_{s})=\bigcup_{b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}}(Y_{b}\setminus V(J_{s}))$.
So, there is a $b\in{\mathchoice{\boldChildrenOp\left(s\right)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}{\boldChildrenOp(s)}}$ with $V(P)\subseteq Y_{b}\cup\{u,v\}$.
By minimality, we even have $u,v\in Y_{b}$.
Now, consider $K\in{\mathchoice{\compOp\left(F[D\cap E(G_{b})]\right)}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}{\compOp(F[D\cap E(G_{b})])}}$ with $u\in V(K)$.
Then, $V(P)\subseteq V(K)$ and in particular $v\in V(K)$.
As $u$ and $v$ are not isolated in $F[D]$, there are $e_{u}\in E(F^{\prime}[D])$ and $e_{v}\in E(F^{\prime}[D])$ adjacent to $u$ and $v$ respectively.
As all edges completely contained in $Y_{b}$ are removed from $F^{\prime}$, we have $e_{u},e_{v}\in E({\mathchoice{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}{\boundaryOp_{b}}})$ and, by definition of $\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}$ and $\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}$, there is a $P\in\pastPartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}\cup\futurePartBaseOp_{\tau_{b}}$ with $e_{u},e_{v}\in P$.
Since $\tilde{p}_{b,P}\in\widetilde{Z}_{D}$ and all adjacent edges are contained in $\widetilde{Z}^{E}_{D}$, $u$ and $v$ are actually in the same component of $\widetilde{F}[D\cup\widetilde{Z}^{E}_{D}]$.
Thus, $\widetilde{F}[D\cup\widetilde{Z}^{E}_{D}]$ is connected.
